# Mesh Provenance Audit

Generated: 2026-06-10

## Scope

- Current site repo: `/Users/mike/Documents/New project 2/atrium` (`never-nude/atrium`).
- Previous site repo: `/tmp/provenance-formgallery-site` clone of `never-nude/formgallery-site`.
- Included extensions: `.stl`, `.obj`, `.mtl`, `.ply`, `.glb`, `.gltf`, `.zip`, plus texture images referenced by the model package search.
- Mesh files were never modified or re-saved; this audit only reads bytes, git history, repo metadata, and public web/source pages.

## Phase 0 - Inventory And Schema

Target provenance schema discovered in `museum/shared/catalog.js`, `museum/shared/catalog-overlay.js`, and `museum/shared/met-batch-20260329.js`:

- `source: { summary, links: [{ label, url }], note }`
- `lobbyMeta` for the visible source summary
- `scan_source`, `mesh_format`, `license` where the catalog already needs structured source details
- `model: { primaryUrl, fallbackUrl }` for local or canonical source mesh URLs
- Piece identity fields such as `viewerTitle`, `subtitle`, `artist`, `culture`, `region`, `period`, and location fields
- Added in this audit: `provenanceConfidence` with values `confirmed` or `probable`; unknown entries are intentionally not filled into site metadata.

Inventory result: 375 asset rows across both repos: 371 mesh rows, 4 texture rows, and 0 archives. Current site has 191 asset rows, including 9 untracked SMK working-tree meshes. Form Gallery has 184 asset rows, including 2 history-only meshes found in git.

## Phase 1 - Forensic Extraction

- Captured SHA256, byte size, original added filename, git add date/message, same-commit siblings, mesh format details, STL headers/triangle counts, OBJ comments/material references, GLB generators, and vertex/face counts where parseable.
- File-level extractor script: `scripts/provenance_inventory.py`.
- Resolved/probable/unknown after extraction plus in-repo metadata: confirmed 361, probable 2, unknown 8 mesh rows.

## Phase 2 - Offline Cross-Reference

- Exact SHA duplicates between current and Form Gallery: 180 unique shared mesh hashes. These inherited provenance from the documented current catalog or from Form Gallery bundled README/catalog history.
- The nine untracked SMK release meshes match `atrium-vault` manifest SHA256 values exactly, so their vault provenance is conclusive.
- Near-match review found no unresolved nonidentical same-title/same-count files requiring a guessed attribution.
- Unique mesh-source status after offline cross-reference and known repo evidence: confirmed 186, probable 1, unknown 4.

## Phase 3 - Web Verification

- Michelangelo David: downloaded the Wikimedia Commons direct STL and matched SHA256/byte size to the local file; source credits Scan the World and lists CC BY-SA 4.0.
- Bouchardon Cupid: current MyMiniFactory download was not available for hash comparison, but Wayback captures of the Scan The World page identify the work and original filename. Marked `probable`, not `confirmed`.
- Wayback CDX checks for `formgallery.org/*` and specific legacy routes returned no useful captures; no provenance was inferred from absent captures.
- Final unique mesh-source status: confirmed 186, probable 1, unknown 4. Final mesh file-row status: confirmed 361, probable 2, unknown 8.
